The template is spec'd in full contract form (world-story-templates.md sec 9: logline, preconditions, roles, threads, mid-turn, endings, coda, arc attachment) and lands at slice scale in the same session, eligible tier 6+ wherever the leaguer stands, extending the ladder the pool already climbs (Raided Stead always, Blight/Throne/Faiths tier 2+, Gold Rush tier 3+). The dramatic question is what you owe a fear that kept you safe. The accepted history is the stead's penned-thing tale, told as gratitude rather than dread: something old and hungry walled under the holm in the first days, and so long as stones fall on the causeway it stays there, a watch the stead never had to post. The evidence is the holm itself, read by standing on the besieged ground (the D-057 map's own geometry: the causeway, the bare holm, the bounds the bare-holm texture already used): hearth-rings and bed-hollows, a child's game of counters by the reed line, and on the far shore a tally of winters and mouths that stops at the winter the mere froze, headed by the same house-mark the stead cuts over its own door-stones. The flip complicates twice, per the iron rule: nothing was ever penned (the horror was never anything but the stead's own founders, who slipped the leaguer across one winter's ice and raised the town in the siege's shadow), and the fear that wrongs them was their own make, told so the siege's setters would never come asking what became of the quarry, which means the stead has spent its whole history thanking the works set to starve its own grandmothers. The cast is one drawn villager, the fisher, whose whole-life wanting (the best water in the valley, never a net wet in it) voices act 1 against the tale and knowing it; the complicit authority is the founders themselves, a corpse-authority in the Gold Rush's surveyor line but with self-protection where the surveyor had kindness. Six storylets in the proven slice shape: plea (promise lift_the_leaguer), NearHouse accepted-history beat, holm evidence (mere_truth), the lifting with the truth in hand held as a scene on D-117's seam ("The ice and the tally": carry the tally down and the gratitude turns into inheritance, coda founding_carried; or leave the founders their fear, coda fear_stands plus a withheld fact), the cold ending without the truth (plain lines, fear_stands), and the kept promise paying the standard 3 essence. The kept truth crosses as a mere_truth silence on D-120's pipe (the penned-thing tale told for true, a new case in the crossing's switch), and endings differ in what the valley believes, never in the coin. Save v71 -> v72: tier-6+ worlds now draw their story from six candidates where a v71 journal drew from five, so every late world's selection and cast re-deals; tier 1-5 worlds are untouched by construction (the leaguer precondition fails, the eligible set is unchanged, and the one-eligible fast path keeps consuming what it always did). Verified the established ways: 698 tests green (nine new SiegeTests mirroring the Rush suite: deterministic tier-6 selection with all six templates surfacing across forty seeds, the plea, the ambient history, the cold ending, the unchecked truth scene, both branches with their codas, the silence crossing with the tale told for true, story-ends-once against a late tally, and the kept promise; one pre-existing LeaguerTests texture test hardened onto a non-siege world, since in a Long Siege world the evidence outranks the bare-holm line by design, the D-121 hardening precedent). Twins byte-identical on all five sweep seeds; all five moved off the v72 baselines exactly as predicted for a re-dealt tier-6+ story draw, and the drift is healthy end to end: every seed still reaches cycle 13 with all 12 crossings, death counts identical on every seed, key counts near-identical except seed 7 (+2839 keys, a different late-world story playing out longer); sim replays exact (seed 1: 16102 keys, cycle 13, turn 12292; seed 7: 16950, 13, 13005; seed 2024: 12398, 13, 11666); the v73 sweeps are the new baselines. Options set aside: the dying god's succession as the sixth spine instead (it wants the institutions' machinery grown further and had no site standing empty-handed, where the leaguer was a built stage waiting; it stays the named seventh candidate); binding the siege to the ringfort (the fort is a watch, not a siege, its deed already reads relief, and its guard-war combat identity just landed in D-129: the leaguer owns the leaguer); a live besieger-remnant NPC voice (the warders and the bare-holm texture already carry the watch's presence, and a talking remnant would out-scale the slice); making the fisher's plea fear-tinged rather than wanting (the wanting is the banked-ordinary register the spec names, and the tension with the tale is sharper from someone who half-disbelieves it). Deferred: the seventh template (the dying god's succession, the research's last named candidate); the storylet-count numbers, player-visible selection, and tier-tilt questions standing in world-story-templates.md sec 11; D-025's economy hooks whenever the opened mere should move fish and prices.

### D-129: The guard war's coda: the board-check and the drilled bind (2026-07-20)
Combat depth's sixth rung, and the two kinds the second bars were built waiting for (deferred by name at D-125 and D-126) ship together, because they are two halves of one closure: the pressure war finally runs both ways through named hands. The board-check is the field's first pressure verb aimed at the bearer's guard and not the blood, and it goes to the shield-carl, the one body that already owns a board and a fort: at arm's length, a quarter of its turns, the whole board squares behind a shoulder as a telegraphed blow at the bearer's cell (a new IntentKind on the same rails as every shown blow), and what lands is thrown mass along the guard's line at the charge's tier (3), no dice and not a drop of blood in it. It rides D-126's reading whole (a pressing bearer is leaned on for 4, a set guard blunts it to 2, a held wind-up deepens it), it is dodged by feet like anything shown, and it is met by the parry, which is deliberately its cleanest counter: a met check hands the carl's own thrown mass back for the parry's full 4, so the guard war's verb and the guard war's answer point at each other. A sundered board (D-095) has no check left in it, the two board systems agreeing; and the board stays in its line through the blow, hit or miss, so the check never opens the archer's window: that price stays the seax's alone. The read names it on the sidebar, and a keen read names what it wants. The drilled thegn is the first kind that answers being parried, D-125's exact deferral: taught the bind beside the blow, its met cut gives the bearer's guard only half its force (2, not the parry's 4) and shoves a point back through the crossed iron, the bind riding the same bearer-side reading (a set guard shrugs the shove whole). The parry against a thegn stays worth declaring on purpose: the blow is still turned bloodless and the guard-work still teaches the family in hand; the bind makes it a trade in the pressure war instead of a free rocking, which is what a drilled hand is. The feint interplay is untouched by construction: a lying mark can never be met (D-125), so the bind only ever answers the honest cut, and the keen read's whole D-096 payoff stands. Save v70 -> v71: the carl's check adds a draw beside every adjacent carl, so a v70 journal's fort fights re-deal from the first threshold held. Verified the established ways: 689 tests green (nine new GuardWarTests, diceless where the design is: the check shoving the guard and not the blood, the stance read on both ends, the dodge by feet, the met check handing back the full 4, the whole board checking and the sundered board never, the drilled bind's half force and returned point, the set guard shrugging the bind, and the undrilled hand still giving its whole swing; one pre-existing RingfortTests seax test hardened to wait past the check for the seax, the D-121 hardening precedent). Twins byte-identical on all five sweep seeds; all five moved off the v70 baselines exactly as predicted for a new draw in standing fights, and the drift is healthy end to end: every seed still reaches cycle 13 with all 12 crossings, deaths equal or fewer (seed 2024's two down to one), hauls near-identical, and seed 1 wears D-126's own signature (2665 more keys, 281 fewer turns, same 1 death: the checked pilot's refused bumps are turn-free and it walks out). Sim replays exact (seed 1: 16081 keys, cycle 13, turn 12289; seed 7: 14111, 13, 13124; seed 2024: 12473, 13, 11730); the v72 sweeps are the new baselines. Options set aside: a new kind invented to carry the pressure verb (the carl already owns the board, the fort, and the threshold; a new body for one verb is roster inflation); the check displacing the bearer a cell (the hound's drag owns dragging, and the check's identity is pure pressure: mixing in movement would blur both); the thegn refusing the parry outright or answering with an instant counter-cut (the parry must stay worth its turn, D-125's whole economy; the bind is a trade, not a refusal, and an extra un-shown blow would break the telegraph contract the parry lives on); exposure on a missed check (the board never leaves its line: the carl's windows are the seax's, D-053's geometry kept); the check at range or on the charge (it is a threshold verb; reach is the boar's and the spear's business). Deferred: parry- and stance-riding knacks (D-094/D-125, still the natural next combat texture); flanking proper and the warder rim after the board breaks (D-095); more second moves and cry-wakes-dormant-bands (D-096); formal duels (vision sec 4).

### D-128: The stead reads its own unease: the secret facts' consumer pass (2026-07-20)
The deferral D-127 named in the same breath it tripled the fuel: the secret-facts consumer pass, one small session in the D-109 discipline (narrative and facts, deliberately no mechanics, because the world reading differently IS the payoff). Of the family's three secrets, lifted_purse already had its reader (the two ledgers, D-109), so this pass pays the two the roadmap tracked open: burgled_house (D-127) and fenced_goods (D-124), and with them every secret fact a deed can write now has a consumer. The bolted dark is the burgled house read off the lane: bright iron on wood gone grey, the yard dog now sleeping behind the door, talk that stops at one threshold, a NearHouse beat once per world at the two-ledgers priority, with the Aegis counting what one crossed sill is costing a stead that spends hardest against the wrong it cannot name. Its one structural idea is the forbid: the beat dies for the world the moment shame/housebroken stands, because a lane that has seen a face come out of a doorway has a name for its trouble, and the beat's whole weight was the lack of one; the clean burglar hears the stead grope in the dark, the caught one never hears this beat at all. The heirloom missed is the fenced goods grieved to the very hand that sent them down the road: a villager shaping a whittled nothing in the air, the floor up twice, "nobody here would take it" said to the taker, and the Aegis naming the one direction a stead cannot imagine (away) and the knowing as the heaviest thing the cart left behind. Gated on live shame at zero like the tale carried, since the confidence is only offered to an unpriced face; villager Talk, once per world. Both beats are pure perception, no Effect, no new state, and no save bump (v70 holds, the D-109 precedent): they ride facts the replay already rebuilds, and being fact-gated on crimes no journey commits they change no draw in any pilot or shipped journal. Verified the established ways: 680 tests green first run (five new in FactConsumerTests: the bolted dark read once and only once after the clean entry, the forbid holding once the lane has a face, both probed honest on seed 1, whose first burgle is clean and second caught from the stead's center; the heirloom grieved to the very hand through the real cart sale, the beat waiting for the sale while the trinket rides the pack, and the confidence withheld from a priced face); twins byte-identical on all five sweep seeds and all five byte-identical to the v70 baselines outright, exactly as predicted; sim replays exact. Options set aside: mechanics on either beat (a watch hired, prices moving, a search of the bearer's pack: the D-109 discipline exists precisely to keep the graph's payoff narrative until a system genuinely wants the hook); a chain beat for both secrets standing together (the two beats already compose in play, and a third telling would spend the same irony twice); the burgled beat gated on live shame like the heirloom's (wrong shape: the lane's iron is not a confidence offered to the bearer, it is the street being read, and a bearer ashamed of something else entirely can still read it). Deferred: a consumer for shame/housebroken in its turn (the named housebreaker meeting the lane that knows, natural fuel beside the thief's reception); the discovery-risk lane if a kept secret should ever be able to come out (tracked since D-109); Sleight knacks (four feeders and a real curve, still waiting).

### D-127: The crossed sill: burglary proper, and the crime family's last named verb (2026-07-20)
The four verbs D-006 named for crime (lockpicking, pickpocketing, burglary, fencing) close with the one deferred longest, at D-107 and again at D-122, both times for wanting "interior furniture and item plumbing" the stead did not have. The answer is that it never needed them: the coffer taught the lock, the lift taught the dice, the mantel taught the heirloom, and the fence taught the buyer, so burglary is the composition of everything the family already built, aimed the whole distance in. The verb is 's' (Command.Burgle, a new journaled key), the family's fourth deliberate key beside 'g' and 'p', pressed beside one of the stead's own doors: the latch slipped, the sill crossed, the dark of a lived-in house crossed to the kist against the wall. The dice ride Sleight alone, one roll for the whole of it (in, through, and out with nobody woken), on a curve seated deliberately between the pocket's and the coffer's (0.40 green, +0.05 per level, capped 0.85: a latch is kinder than old iron, but old iron does not breathe in the loft). One try per door per world on the family's one-sitting idiom, because a house that has heard the step listens harder after; the ledger is the house's own and independent of the pilfer ledger, since a reached-over sill says nothing about the kist behind it. The clean entry is the family's biggest home take and its quietest: 4-9 coin (over the lift and the sill, under the guilt-free coffer, because a home's kist holds a home's savings, not a payroll), plus an heirloom into the trinket pocket for the peddler's fence (D-124's loop closed from the far end: rob the inside of a door, walk to the cart, come back with clean coin), the stead's third secret fact from a deed (secret/burgled_house, joining lifted_purse and fenced_goods in the consumer pass the roadmap owes), Sleight fed, and no shame at all: burglary done right is the first crime against the stead's own that the stead cannot see, which is exactly its design identity against pilfering (always seen, "steads count their loaves"). The caught entry is the family's worst: a floorboard, a voice, a face seen against the lane, and the shame jumps two rungs in one night (RaiseShame(2), the unified ladder held: a loaf off a sill is hunger, a body in the dark of your house is something else), writing shame/housebroken beside the rung facts. Restitution keeps D-086's whole symmetry at double stakes: the same key at the same door, twice a sill's coin (BreakInRepayCoin 12, the stead pricing trust by how far in the hand went), bringing both rungs back down, with making right outranking more wrong at the corner. Save v69 -> v70: 's', once nothing, now draws dice and moves coin, shame, heirlooms, and skill. Verified the established ways: 675 tests green (ten new BurglaryTests on the same pinned seeds as the lift's, probed once and stable: seed 1 clean, seed 4 caught; the odds seated between pocket and coffer, the clean entry's coin, heirloom, secret, skill, and silence, the caught entry's two rungs and both facts, the one-door sitting, the repay walking both rungs down and closing the door both ways, short coin leaving the wrong standing, the sill and kist as two ledgers, no hearths below ground, no door costing no turn, and the crossing regenerating the doors innocent); twins byte-identical on all five sweep seeds; baselines byte-identical to v69 on all five (the pilot never presses 's': the honest-bearer path, pilot-unexercised by design, test-covered, the D-107/D-122/D-124 precedent); sim replays exact (seed 1: 13416 keys, cycle 13, turn 12570; seed 7: 16523, 13, 12605; seed 2024: 12581, 13, 11838); the v70 sweeps are the new baselines. Options set aside: interior maps or furniture plumbing (the whole finding is that the family's pieces compose; a room the player walks would be a different game's burglary and is not owed); a witness/heat system beyond the one roll (the roll is the witness: splitting entry, search, and exit into stages multiplies dice without multiplying decision); the take as rations or gear (the kist holds savings and heirlooms; the loaf is the sill's, and gear identity belongs to the chests, D-041); burgling the songhall, larder, or harrow (institutions keep watch by nature, and each already has its own economy; the three homes are the verb's honest ground); night-gating (the game keeps no clock of hours, and inventing one for a gate would be machinery for flavor). Deferred: the secret-facts consumer pass, now three strong (lifted_purse, fenced_goods, burgled_house: the stead reading its own unease); Sleight knacks (the skill now has four feeders and a real curve); deeper crime texture (a nemesis constable, organized work) if the family ever wants a fifth verb it was never promised.

### D-126: The bearer's second bar: the field reads you back (2026-07-20)
Combat depth's fifth rung, and the last asymmetry in D-004's contract closes: for a hundred and twenty-five decisions the field telegraphed to the bearer and never once read the bearer back. Both halves of the remaining clause ship together, because each is the other's delivery: the bearer's own posture bar (Will's posture clause, dormant since D-015 set the seven attributes) and monsters reading the bearer's stance and commitment (deferred at D-058, which shipped the thegn's heave-counter as the first reader and left the rest standing). The bar: the bearer's guard is rocked only by landed, telegraphed, committed blows, the field's own D-014 mirror (a nip, a bite, an untelegraphed trade is the field's winded tap and rocks nothing; stones and the lofted cast are thrown weight against the body, not force through the guard's line, and the cry and the cold are not blows). Light blows rock a point (the club, the barrow blade, the seax, the measured cut), heavy blows two (the severed one's cut, the graven fist, the hound's full-length lunge), the boar's charge three by sheer mass, the heave's mirror. The brim is Will's at last: 8 at the humble baseline (a carl's discipline, between the carl's 8 and the wight's 6 on the same scale the foes wear), one more per point of Will above it, the posture clause paid in the stat's own words. The reading is where the other half lives, deterministic and legible, no new dice anywhere: committed force works a pressing bearer's thinner guard a point harder and a set guard a point less (a guarded bearer shrugs a light blow whole: the turtle's identity, and the anti-facetank rule's honest counterweight), and a body holding its own wind-up (heave or levin) is a body whose guard is already down, so the blow that finds it rocks a point deeper: every kind now punishes commitment through pressure where D-058 gave only the thegn its outright break. At the brim the bearer's guard breaks, the foes' stagger turned honestly around: the held heave dies in the bearer's own hands, the posture resets, and for two full turns the arms refuse (swing, thrust, heave, and parry all turn-free refusals) while every blow finds the open guard 2 deeper, but the feet keep working, deliberately half the foes' sentence: a staggered monster is a target, a staggered bearer is a decision, and retreat is the whole answer. The thegn alone knows the door the break leaves: its cut into a beaten-open guard takes 2 more atop the open guard's 2, the riposte's exact mirror (flat 4) in the one hand drilled to see it, announced in its own line. Quiet ground settles the worn bar whole (fight pressure, not a wound: the mirror of a foe's bar dying with the foe), and death and the crossing clear it with everything else held. The sidebar carries both ends: the worn count while it climbs, GUARD OPEN in red while the stagger runs; the snapshot gained GuardWorn and StaggerTurns beside WoundedTurns. Save v68 -> v69: the hardest fights resolve differently, and a refused swing spends no turn where a v68 one spent it. Verified the established ways: 665 tests green (thirteen new BearerGuardTests, diceless where the design is: the brim as Will's clause, the shown blow rocking and the stone striking only the body, the pressing foot leaned on and the set guard shrugging, the committed body read, the brim breaking the guard and the heave dying in hand, the staggered arms refusing all four verbs turn-free while the feet still answer, the open guard taking blows exactly 2 deeper and the thegn's point exactly 4, the charge's mass, quiet ground settling the bar, and the fall handing the bearer back standing); no existing test moved. Twins byte-identical on all five sweep seeds; four of five byte-identical to v68 outright (the pilot dodges marked cells by doctrine, so landed committed blows are rare enough that no brim of 8 fills on those roads); seed 7 drifts honestly and tells the mechanic's whole story in three numbers: 2545 more keys, 397 fewer turns, same 3 deaths (the pilot's guard broke in the deep fights, its bumps were refused turn-free while it stood armless, and it walked out of every one of them alive). Sim replays exact (seed 1: 13416 keys, cycle 13, turn 12570; seed 7: 16523, 13, 12605; seed 2024: 12581, 13, 11838); the v69 sweeps are the new baselines. Options set aside: the stagger holding the bearer's feet like a foe's (a lost turn is a lost fight at 20 HP; the armless retreat is the same lesson without the coin flip on the bearer's life); dice on the pressure or the break (the second bar's whole register is flat and legible, D-125's ruling held); posture regeneration per turn in-fight (the quiet-ground settle is the only forgiveness, the per-fight shape both bars now share); the brim scaling with Vigor or level besides Will (the clause names Will, and one attribute owning one bar is the legibility); the stone and the loft as pressure (a guard is beaten open along its line; thrown weight bruises the body it lands on); and new reader kinds in the same stroke (the thegn's door-eye and the universal commitment-pressure are the reading; a kind built around guard-breaking wants its own pressure verb and its own session). Deferred: a kind with a pressure verb of its own (a board-check or shoulder aimed at the bearer's bar, not the blood); a kind that answers being parried (D-125, still, the drilled thegn first); parry- and stance-riding knacks (D-094, still); the guard drawn as a true bar in the sidebar if the counts ever want it; and Wounded or the scars leaning on a broken guard (a maimed body's guard giving earlier is a real idea for the cure roads to price).

### D-125: The second bar and the parry: the guard beaten open (2026-07-20)
Combat depth's fourth rung, and the two oldest unstarted boxes in vision sec 4 close together because each is the other's payoff: the posture bar (D-004's own launch-module clause, deferred since the first combat decision) and parry as a distinct verb. Every foe now carries a guard beside its blood (Monster.PostureDmg against a per-kind MaxPosture), rocked by pressure that is not blood, in D-094's flat legible register: a paid blow rocks a point (swing, thrust, or heave's landing all count; a winded tap is free and rocks nothing, D-014's cost gating on the second bar too), the wall hands a shove back for two (paying D-095's tracked brawling wall-slam cost: ground that will not give returns the blow's weight through the body), the heave's sheer mass rocks three, and a parried blow four, the striker's own committed force turned back on it. The brims run 4 to 10 by discipline and mass, not blood: a goblin's guard is habit (4), a carl's is a wall's (8), stone is rocked last (10). At the brim the guard breaks: the wind-up dies unthrown, the body staggers open two turns riding D-053's standing-open machinery (no feet, no blows, shafts find it), extended to the generic kinds that never had it, and the riposte's door opens: one paid melee blow through it lands a flat 4 deeper and spends it; a stagger walked off un-riposted is a door closed, the body next acts whole. The parry is 'a', the read's aggressive answer: against an adjacent foe whose wind-up's shown mark sits on the bearer's own cell and falls this coming turn, the turn is committed to the guard instead of the kill for 2 wind (cheaper than a swing; the blow forgone is the real price), and the blow is turned aside whole, no dice: the declaration's gate is the read itself, which is why the thegn's feint keeps every tooth D-096 gave it: the iron goes where the eye says, a lying mark shows a neighbor cell, and a blow not shown at your ground can never be met (below a keen read the feint is unparryable by construction; at keen the mark is honest and the parry stands, the read tiers paying again at the top). A met parry trains the family in hand (guard-craft is weapon-craft, bare knuckles included), no mark or winded arms refuse without the turn, and the sidebar names both ends of the loop: the blow shown at your feet with the key, and the stagger while the door stands. Save v67 -> v68: long fights resolve differently once guards can break. Verified the established ways: 652 tests green (eleven new PostureTests, diceless where the design is: the guard wearing under paid blows and breaking at the brim, the break stilling the wind-up, the riposte's flat 4 spending its one-blow door, the carl's spent board earning shafts but never the riposte, the parry turning the shown blow whole and rocking the guard its full brim, the no-mark and neighbor-mark refusals, the feint's lying mark unmeetable, the winded arms, the wall handing the shove back, the heave's weight, and the stagger holding feet and blows with the door closing unspent); no existing test moved. Twins byte-identical on all five sweep seeds; four of five byte-identical to v67 outright, because the pilot never presses 'a' (the 'x' precedent) and its fights mostly end before a brim of 4 fills, which is D-004's own pacing promise (trash dies in 2-3 blows) reading back out of the data; seed 1 drifts honestly by 3 keys (same 12570 turns, same 1 death, same 68 chests, coin a hair apart: one long fight shortened by a break). Sim replays exact (seed 1: 13416 keys, cycle 13, turn 12570; seed 7: 13978, 13, 13002; seed 2024: 12581, 13, 11838); the v68 sweeps are the new baselines. Options set aside: the bearer's own posture bar in the same stroke (Will's posture clause, D-015: the foes have no pressure verbs yet, and the bearer's stagger wants its own pass beside monsters-reading-stance, D-058); dice or a skill check on the parry (the gate is the read and the wind; dice would blur the flat register, and the cost is the turn not spent killing); parry as insurance against the feint (rejected above: it would refund the keen read's whole D-096 payoff); a riposte multiplier (flat 4 sits beside D-094's flat 2s and never outruns gear); posture regeneration per turn (fights are short; the bar is per-fight pressure, and the reset at the break is the only forgiveness); parrying stones, charges, cries, and the cold (not blows: the board turns stones and feet answer charges). Deferred: the bearer's own guard bar and the kinds that could break it; a kind that answers being parried (the drilled thegn as the natural first); the guard drawn as a bar in the sidebar if the log lines ever want help; parry- and stance-riding knacks (tracked since D-094).

### D-124: The peddler on the road: the fence, goods with a past, and the economy's first traveling vendor (2026-07-20)
The crime family's oldest named blocker closes: fencing wanted a fence (D-107), and the peddler is it, picked over a second settlement (a whole worldgen pillar for what one cart teaches) and over waiting (the family had three verbs and still no buyer). Two halves, because a fence needs stolen goods worth carrying: pilfering now pockets a small thing off the mantel beside the loaf (Player.Trinket, on the body like hides, surviving death and the crossing), the take a stead would know on sight, which is exactly why no counter inside it will ever buy the thing back; and the peddler stands camped with a cart on every world's overworld (NpcKind.Peddler, own derived stream after every existing draw, on open ground 7-14 out from the stead and clear of the Unbinder's fire), the road's own trader whose whole voice is not asking. The cart's counter is three talk digits: bread at the road's flat price (6 coin, sold to anyone, because the larder's bars and the levy are the stead's books and the cart keeps none: the outcast's grocer, named aloud once to a barred bearer: it prices bread, not conduct), hides at a coin over the wood's-edge bench (HidePriceHere on the HerbPriceHere idiom: D-025's arbitrage, first stone: the same good at two prices in one world), and the fence's digit, the one entry no stead counter has: everything with a past sold in one motion at 7 coin apiece, no dice, no shame, no witness, and the stead's second secret fact from a deed (secret/fenced_goods, beside D-107's lifted purse) written for the consumer the roadmap now owes. The fencing loop is real: rob a door, walk out to the cart, come back with clean coin and a heavier conscience the stead cannot read. The peddler is deliberately not a mark ('p' finds no pocket there: the family does not eat its own infrastructure) and not a villager (no storylet reader mistakes the cart for the lane). Save v66 -> v67: a new person stands on the overworld, so a v66 journal's step onto that tile now bumps to talk, and the cart's digits answer where nothing did. Verified the established ways: 641 tests green (twelve new PeddlerTests, no probing needed: the fence is diceless: the cart in every tier, the talk and its three digits, bread to anyone including the barred thief, the short-coin slate, the pilfered trinket and the full pack taking neither, the fence's pay-quiet-and-fact, the empty pack, the label reading true, the hides' road coin, the unliftable cart, and the trinket crossing worlds to a fresh uncurious cart); twins byte-identical on all five sweep seeds; sim replays exact (seed 1: 13419 keys, cycle 13, turn 12570; seed 7: 13978, cycle 13, 13002; seed 2024: 12581, cycle 13, 11838). The baselines moved off v64 for the first time in three sessions, exactly as predicted for a standing person: four seeds differ only in the key stream itself (equal key counts, byte-identical outcome lines: the pilot wove an equal-length path around the cart), and seed 7 drifts honestly (36 fewer keys, same 12 crossings, same 3 deaths, near-identical hauls); the v67 sweeps are the new baselines. One found-and-fixed: the talk-menu dispatch gates offers by NPC kind, and the peddler was not in the gate, so the cart's digits fell through to the menu close until the kind joined the list. Options set aside: a second settlement as the fence (above); the peddler wandering on coarse ticks (NPC movement is D-031's open item; a camped cart teaches the economy without it); trinkets from the coffer or the lift (the coffer's coin is D-122's verified shape, and the lift's clean take is coin by design; the mantel is where heirlooms live); a fence price haggle or Sleight check (the fence's service is certainty; the dice live on the taking side); regard or shame moving on a fence sale (the whole point is the stead cannot see the road). Deferred: a consumer for secret/fenced_goods (the heirloom missed, a villager reader); the peddler's stock growing with tiers (exotic goods, a second arbitrage leg per D-025); the cart as caravan seed once trading proper opens; a peddler topic on the stead's lane (the wanderer fact is written and waits).

### D-123: The standing round and the light purse read: town life's second verb and the loss ledger's consumer (2026-07-20)
Town life thickens by both of D-108's named deferrals in one small lane. The standing round is carousing's verb, kept deliberately small and kept at the same hearth: one always-listed digit after the bones (state-read label, D-041's law), 5 coin once per world, because one round an evening is generosity and two is campaigning. What it buys is exactly not regard: D-108 set carousing aside because a coin-for-regard dial cheapens the honest roads, so the round moves no rung and opens no ledger, and pays instead in an evening's warmth (the horns down the benches, the old song about the miller's fence), a game/round_stood fact, and a reader on the lane: the-round-remembered, a villager Talk storylet in the D-088 discipline (nothing gained and nothing needing to be: the stead remembers who poured, a short list the bearer is now on, and the Aegis marks the arithmetic aloud: five coin, no rung moved, some of what a stead keeps is not kept in books). A round stood over a board that has been emptying the bearer's purse all evening gets its own toast (character or stubbornness, the room drinks to both). The light purse read is the game/light_purse fact's consumer, the stead reading the fleeced bearer: the-light-purse, a villager Talk storylet gated on the live net at nine down exactly as the lucky hand's talk is gated at nine up, so coin won back across the board ends the reading while the fact stays history, the D-108 symmetry completed on both signs. Dry sympathy and no mechanics: the skald plays fair and wins anyway, that is what a house is, and the one lesson costs what it costs. The RoundStood flag is world state on the BonesNet pattern, replay-rebuilt, reset at the crossing (the next hearth has met no one's generosity). Save v65 -> v66: the round's digit is a new always-listed entry where a v65 key fell through as nothing, and the two storylets enter the talk draws. Verified the established ways: 629 tests green (eight new CarouseTests on pinned seeds, probed once and stable: seed 15 loses its first three games and wins the fourth, covering the reading while it stands and its end when the coin comes back; the round's warmth, fact, and once-only, the short purse, the light-purse toast, the lane's memory, and the crossing forgetting the evening); twins byte-identical; sim replay exact (seed 2024: 12581 keys, cycle 13, turn 11838); baselines byte-identical to v64/v65 on all five sweep seeds, predicted and confirmed (the pilot never gambles or carouses, and both storylets sit behind facts no journey writes: pilot-unexercised by design, test-covered, the D-107/D-108 precedent). Options set aside: the round paying regard or a discount (the whole point of its design is that it does not); a carousing scene or round-standing inside the bones board (the digit law places it beside the game, not inside it); a repeatable round with diminishing warmth (once per world keeps the gesture a gesture; a dial would make it a pump); reading the light purse at the hearth itself (the skald already speaks at the board; the stead's reading belongs on the lane, where the lucky hand's does). Deferred: tournaments, trading, property (infrastructure first, as before); a second game or higher-stakes table deeper in the chain; a consumer for round_stood beyond the lane if a template ever wants a generous bearer.

### D-122: The locked coffer: lockpicking in the fighting deeps, the crime family's guilt-free outlet (2026-07-20)
The crime family's third verb, and the exact shape D-107 deferred it in: lockpicking with deep-site coffers as the guilt-free outlet, picked for this session over fencing (still wants a fence: no peddler or second settlement exists yet) and burglary proper (new interior furniture and item plumbing; the coffer teaches the same lock lesson on furniture that already fits a site). One box of old iron stands in each fighting deep whose makers were the locking kind (goblin camp, quarry, hall, ringfort, leaguer): the barrow is left out on purpose because the dead lock nothing, they watch, and D-106 already owns that ledger; the quiet sites keep no strongboxes. Placement rides its own derived stream per site ("coffer-" + id) drawn after every existing draw, the graven-stone pattern, so pinned worlds keep their layouts and only gain a shut lid at mid-depth or deeper, never on the chest, the stone, or a spawn. The verb is 'g' on the cell, the dice ride Sleight alone on a harder curve than a pocket (Locks.ChanceFor: 0.35 green, +0.06 per level, capped 0.85, because old iron argues and no hand opens everything), and the sitting is single: a lock that gives pays 7-14 coin and feeds the hand, a lock that holds teaches nothing and keeps its lid the rest of the world, one try per lock per world on the one-try-per-pocket idiom. No shame, no facts, no witness either way: whoever owned this iron stopped needing it an age ago, which is what makes it the outlet, and it puts a Sleight surface in the world from tier 1 on that asks no crime against the stead, so the skill can grow clean before its knacks are worth questioning. The camp coffer diegesis keeps the goblins honest: a road-wagon strongbox dragged home whole, because no goblin has the patience for iron that argues. Save v64 -> v65: 'g' on the coffer's cell now draws lock dice and moves coin and skill where a v64 grab fell through as nothing. Verified the established ways: 621 tests green (nine new CofferTests on pinned seeds 1 and 4, probed once and stable: the odds ladder, the placement and the left-out sites, the give paying and teaching, the hold teaching nothing, the single sitting, the emptied box falling through, no ledger either way, the tile's two descriptions, and the crossing regenerating the lock innocent); twins byte-identical; sim replay exact (seed 1: 13419 keys, cycle 13, turn 12570); baselines byte-identical to v64 on all five sweep seeds, predicted and confirmed, because the pilot never tries a lock: the honest-bearer path, pilot-unexercised by design, covered by tests (the D-107/D-108 precedent). Options set aside: fencing and burglary leading (above, both stay on the roadmap); retryable locks (grind until open makes the dice decorative; one sitting keeps them real); a separate lockpicking skill (the family's whole voice is the one light hand, and Sleight needs feeding, not a sibling); coffers in the barrow (the grudge machinery would double-trigger or need an exception, and the fiction is cleaner: the dead keep watch, not locks); loot beyond coin (the signature gear is the chest's own identity, D-041, and a second gear stream would dilute it). Deferred: fencing once a fence exists; burglary proper; Sleight knacks (the skill now has two feeders and a real curve coming); a lockpick tool or quality tier if locks ever want more texture than dice.

### D-121: The Gold Rush: the fifth template, bound to the old quarry (2026-07-20)
The pool's fifth spine and the first new template since the launch list closed (D-116), picked over the other two research candidates (the long siege, the dying god's succession) because it is the only one with an unclaimed site already generated and waiting: the old quarry (tier 3+, D-040), whose "template-driven quarry dressing" deferral this cashes, and whose existing texture (the downed tools set down in rows, the deed-line "set to watch a working no one finished") the template canonizes without contradicting. Eligible tier 3+ where the quarry stands, so tier-1/2 worlds keep their pools and the mid-band, which had the same four templates as tier 2, finally deepens on descent. The spine at slice scale, six storylets: the accepted history is the greed-tale (the graven figures as the old crew, turned to stone mid-stroke over the seam they would not leave: a moral that keeps the pit shut), planted at compile and voiced near the houses; the prospector (drawn villager) asks the bearer to put the watch down (promise open_the_pit, settled for the standard 3 essence, truth-indifferent: the asking was the opening, not the digging); the survey-marks at the working face (X >= 24, past the chest) flip it, and the flip complicates twice: the crew was not punished but struck the seam, read that it runs through the stone that holds the valley's slope, and authored the greed-tale on themselves as a fence, so the wrong story is a kind one, and the wealth is real. The hushing with the truth in hand opens the scene "The fence and the seam" (D-117 seam, no check: nothing in the pit resists): carry the survey down (coda pit_fenced_true: the fence rebuilt on the truth, the rush dies awake) or leave the founders' fence as they built it (coda fence_alone plus withheld pit_truth, the fourth writer into the D-118/D-120 silence pipe, with its own pit_truth retelling arm at the crossing: the pit that ate its greedy crew, told for true). Without the truth the ending stays plain lines (fence_alone: the watch falls, the tale weakens, and no one knows what the founders knew). Save v63 -> v64: tier-3+ worlds draw among five templates instead of four and re-deal their spines. 612 tests green (RushTests new with nine, master seed 2's cycle-3 world; two pre-existing tests in HallTests/LeaguerTests hardened against the shuttered-window scene the re-dealt NearHouse draw can open mid-loop). Journey baselines legitimately moved on all five sweep seeds (the re-deal), twins byte-identical, sim replay exact, all reaching cycle 13; the whole lane confirmed live on seed 2 (greed-tale voiced, plea heard, survey read, scene answered with the survey carried down). Deferred: the buyer/factor role and a live rush thread (arrivals, prices) with D-025's economy hooks; the sixth template (long siege or dying god's succession); the war-profiteer and escalation beats as before.

### D-120: The count travels: the unsaid crosses as a silence (2026-07-20)
The deferral D-118 named and D-119 left with three writers waiting: the withheld
fact gets its cross-world consumer, and the design rule is that the unsaid crosses
on its own legs. A truth kept at its saying-moment (the faiths' claim, the blight's
stilling, the throne's fall) left a wrong story standing, and a standing story
travels the way any good story does: the crossing captures every withheld fact and
presses it into the next world as a silence fact whose detail is the wrong story
told for true (the barrow outlasted, the seat avenged, the stone given), and every
later crossing carries the whole count forward, so the silences accumulate for the
rest of the run. Deliberately not hushed-gated, on both the pipe and the surfacing:
the hushed name stills the songs about the bearer, and the story a kept truth left
standing was never one of those; you cannot hush what was never said (also the
mechanical honesty call: an oath must not be a guilt-eraser). One consumer ships:
silence-retold, a NearHouse storylet, once per world, a walker off the trade-road
telling the story from over the arch while the stead leans in, and the Aegis naming
the one person under the sky who could say otherwise ("I keep the count of unsaid
things, and the count travels"). r0 is the oldest silence in the count. Save v62 ->
v63 (the retelling shifts the storylet draw a v62 journal never saw). 603 tests
green (SilenceTests new with five: the pressing, the clean crossing, accumulation
across arches, the hushed arch carrying it anyway, and the retelling once per
world; BlightTests +1 end to end: the kept truth answered in the scene crosses as
a silence). The pilot always answers scenes first-choice and so never keeps a
truth: journey baselines byte-identical to v62 on all five sweep seeds, twins
byte-identical, sim replay exact, all reaching cycle 13. Options set aside: gating
the pipe on the hushed name (rejected above); a talk-topic or songhall surfacing
(the verse-wall sings the bearer's deeds; this story is nobody's deed, it belongs
in a stranger's mouth at a doorway); pressing codas generally across worlds (the
silences are the one thread the bearer chose to own; a general coda pipe is
mythology-weight work, still deferred from D-116). Deferred: a fifth template;
the war-profiteer and escalation beats; deeper silence consumers (the count read
back at the arc's late rungs would be the natural place, when the arc gets there).
### D-119: The hill and the cairn: the blight's and the throne's climaxes staged as scenes (2026-07-20)
The lane D-118 opened, finished: the two remaining plot-beat climaxes convert to
scenes, so every template's truth-in-hand ending is now a held moment instead of
a fact-branch. Both endings fire on DeedWritten, mid-site, the moment the last
foe falls; InScene routes ahead of everything, so the scene plays over the site
with no plumbing change, the same free ride the faiths' Rest hook got. The
template contract grows the same way in both: the ending used to branch on
whether the truth was found before the deed; found, it now branches on what the
finder does with it. The blight's cb-ending-truth becomes "What comes down the
hill": the stilling done, the stead a scatter of lit doorways below, and two
answers, carry the paid watch's truth down (truth_published, the old ending's
line word for word) or leave the telling under the turf (truth_buried, plus
withheld/mound_truth: the curse-story survives by choice now, not ignorance).
The throne's ut-ending-truth becomes "The cairn and the ledger": the fires out,
the cairn passed once more on the way down, and two answers, carry the ledger
down (seat_truth_carried, old line preserved) or leave the truth under the
cairn (seat_lie_stands, plus withheld/seat_truth). No checks in either scene,
deliberately: the dens are dead and nothing on either hill resists, and dice
against no resistance would be a roll wearing a choice's clothes (the faiths'
check had a live listener to persuade; these moments are bare). Every leaf
writes story_complete in OnEnter, ends-once (D-112) holds on all roads, and the
no-truth endings stay plain lines as before (nothing to choose). The throne's
settling now follows the choice, not the evidence: ut-telling-truth forbids
withheld/seat_truth, and a ninth storylet, ut-telling-kept, is the game's first
withheld consumer: the teller closes the book content while its one reader
stands by saying nothing, a heavier quiet than not knowing, and it pays the
same 3 essence. Late-read evidence carries no withheld fact, so the late truth
still reaches the teller exactly as D-112 documented. The blight's settling
stays truth-indifferent on purpose: the afflicted asked for the stilling, not
the history. Save v61 -> v62 (a v61 journal reaching either climax with the
evidence read replays into a modal choice that did not exist). 597 tests green
(BlightTests and ThroneTests each grew by two). Unlike the faiths' talk-gated
climax, the pilot walks these roads: it reads the deep stones, clears the
sites, and answers the scenes (first choice, so it carries the truth), so the
journey baselines legitimately moved for the first time since D-116: on all
five sweep seeds turns are identical to v61 and keys grew by exactly the
scene answers (6 to 12 keys), twins byte-identical, sim replay exact, all
reaching cycle 13. Options set aside: a third checked answer in either scene
(the faiths' wielding needed a listener; a shaped half-telling here has no one
to resist it until the settling, and rolling now against resistance later is
dishonest staging); a withheld-routed settling for the blight (the promise was
end_the_creep, kept either way; routing it on the history would claim the
afflicted asked a question they never asked). Deferred: a cross-world withheld
consumer (a later world's echo reading the silences, three writers now waiting
on it); a fifth template; the war-profiteer and escalation beats as before.

### D-118: The claim at dawn: the faiths' climax staged as a scene (2026-07-20)
The first plot-beat conversion on the D-117 machinery, and the exact deferral both
D-116 and D-117 named first: the War of Faiths' truth-in-hand climax becomes a
dialogue scene, and the three-way ending the full-scale spec sketches (publish,
suppress, side with one faith) is now a player choice at slice scale. wf-claim-truth
keeps its gating untouched (Rest hook, both accounts heard, evidence in hand,
story_complete forbidden); what grew is delivery: the storylet's lines moved into
"The claim at dawn", opening on the elder saying the claim and the held breath
after it, with the socket's telling the bearer's to spend or keep. Three answers.
Say it whole: the war that never starts, one_grief_shared, the old best-ending
prose word for word, because that ending was already right. Turn the cuts against
the harrow's claim: the game's second visible check (Presence, difficulty 1) and
its first with stakes beyond flavor; carried, the half-truth lands the way a blade
lands and the claim breaks (new coda claim_broken: the stead keeps stone, book,
and last word, nothing is shared, and the whole truth stays cut where no one now
will climb to read it); seen through, the elder (honest to the bone, and it cuts
both ways) refuses a reason built to order, the truth is spent from the bearer's
mouth, and the quarrel shelves on the old answers (claim_shelved). Keep the
telling: the spec's suppression at slice scale; the valley ends exactly where the
cold climax ends (claim_shelved), but the graph remembers why, via the new
`withheld` fact type (the founding truth stayed in its stone by choice, not
ignorance), fuel for a future consumer. Every leaf writes story_complete in its
OnEnter, so the ends-once rule (D-112) holds on all roads. A third settling joined
for the broken claim: the straddler watched what you did and has not decided what
they watched, and it pays the same 3 essence, because endings differ in what the
valley believes, never in the pay. Without the truth there is nothing to choose,
so wf-claim-cold deliberately stays plain lines: a scene with one real answer
would be a menu wearing a moment's clothes. Eleven faiths storylets now. Save
v60 -> v61 (a v60 journal reaching this climax replays into a modal choice that
did not exist, and the wielding draws on the combat stream). One test hook added
(Debug_BurnCombatRoll) so both wielding branches are provable from the pinned
faiths master. 593 tests green (FaithsTests grown from 9 to 13). Twins
byte-identical and journals replay exact through sim on all five sweep seeds, all
reaching cycle 13 and byte-identical to the v60 baselines, the honest expectation:
no worldgen or selection change, and the climax sits behind talk beats the pilot
never walks (pilot-unexercised and test-covered per the D-109/D-111 precedent).
Options set aside: staging the no-truth climax as a terminal scene (nothing to
choose); letting the wielding side with the harrow as well (the claim is the
harrow's own; confirming it from the stead's shrine ground had no honest staging
at slice scale, and one wielding direction proves the pattern). Deferred: the
blight and throne climax conversions; a withheld consumer (a later world's echo
reading the silence); the war-profiteer and escalation beats as before.

### D-117: Dialogue-tree scenes with visible skill checks (2026-07-20)
D-021's oldest open line cashed, and the seam design/storylets.md sec 6 promised is
paid exactly as written: gating did not change, delivery grew. A storylet can now
carry a `Scene`, and when it fires the beat plays as a modal dialogue tree instead
of log lines alone: nodes of prose over numbered answers, branching on the digits,
with entry effects writing facts as ever. The machinery is the talk-menu pattern
made narrative: scene state lives on Game behind `InScene`, every key routes
through ApplyKey and lands in the journal, so a save mid-scene replays back into
the same open moment and the whole layer stays seed-deterministic. The prose lands
in the log as it plays, so the log remains the game's one full transcript, and
scene lines expand the same captures and templates storylet lines do. The visible
check is the genuinely new surface, the first in the game: a checked choice shows
its odds on the choice row before the player commits ("Presence, 40 in 100"), read
off the bearer's own sheet at node entry, and the roll at commit is exactly the
number shown, drawn on the gameplay stream. The formula is the Lifting family's:
half for an unremarkable bearer, a twentieth per point of skill level or attribute
over baseline, a tenth off per difficulty step, clamped to [0.05, 0.95] so every
check stays a real one. Skill checks feed the skill on success; the first shipped
check is Presence, which wakes the seventh and last inert attribute. One modal
rule is deliberately not the menus': while choices stand, every other key is the
scene waiting. A scene is a moment, not a menu; walking away is an authored
answer, priced in content, never a free escape (the option to close-on-any-key
like the talk menu was considered and rejected on exactly this ground). A node
with no choices is terminal and closes on any key. First content: grievance-voiced
grown into "The shuttered window": press the villager for the whole of it (the
checked pressing: the shutter swings wide and writes counsel/camp_ways, or the
bolt slides home), give your word (promise/quiet_nights), or leave them to their
evening; the met fact and the gratitude chain stand untouched. The pilot answers
every scene with its first choice and closes terminals with a space, so journeys
exercise the checked branch live. Save v59 -> v60 (the scene's digits are new
journaled keys and every check shifts the combat stream). 590 tests green (7 new
ScenesTests: the visible tag, both branches across twenty steads, the promise,
leaving-stays-spent, the moment waiting, the odds clamps, and a full journal
replay through the real wake). Twins byte-identical and journals replay exact
through sim on all five sweep seeds, all reaching cycle 13; the window fired live
seven times in the master's twelve worlds, three carries and four fails, both
endings live. Options set aside: a dedicated scene RNG stream (the combat stream
is the one gameplay stream and the journal makes replay exact either way); scene
directions inside `Lines` (a typed field keeps the format honest and maps 1:1 to
a future data file). Deferred: converting the standing plot beats (the faiths'
claim at the shrine first, whose full three-way ending D-116 explicitly parked on
this machinery, then the blight and throne climaxes); checks whose consequences
alter simulation; ask-about topics surfaced inside scenes; a scene-native frame
for the templates' witnessed endings.

### D-116: The War of Faiths: the fourth template, cast by office on D-115's institutions (2026-07-20)
D-114's second lane lands and the named launch-template list closes: the War of
Faiths compiles at slice scale through the D-032/D-035 compiler seam (template 3 of
design/story/world-story-templates.md sec 7), on the institutions D-115 raised. The
casting principle is new to the pool and now named in the context's contract: the
whole cast holds offices, not lots. The shrinekeeper and the harrow's elder are the
two believer-champions, the doorward is the keeper-of-the-founding-site who knows
and has kept silent, and only two draws happen at compile, the straddler (a villager
of harrow kin who prays both ways, the spec's war-orphan/convert at stead scale) and
which side did this season's wrong. The compile context grew a full-cast list to
make that possible (Villagers stays the only drawable pool), and the story compile
moved below the faiths' casting in worldgen so the templates see the whole standing
cast; it draws only from its own stream and the villagers-only pool is unchanged, so
every cast-by-lot role lands where it always did.

**The war at slice scale is a feud not yet bled, per the spec's own preconditions.**
The aggressor designation (the spec's cure for both-sides mush) is a compile draw:
one side has stopped arguing and started taking (kerb-stones carted off the ring, or
the year's offerings taken off the shrine-stone), and the wrong is voiced by the
straddler and at the doors. The two schism accounts are the paired accepted-history:
the same parting told against each other (jealousy swearing a loan into the record,
versus a theft dressed in thanks), each champion voicing its own and the elder,
honest to the bone, asking the bearer to bring a reason to doubt it. The evidence
sits where D-115 built the room to hold it: stepping onto the mother-stone reads the
empty socket's cuts, and the truth complicates both books instead of crowning either
(two keepers of one rite, a burying winter, the stone carried down to stand over the
stead's dying; lent and given both invented after). The climax cashes D-115's rumor
line literally: the elder comes down and says the claim at the shrine, staged on the
Rest hook (rest is shrine-only, so the stage is free), gated on having heard both
accounts. Truth in hand, the claim dissolves into shared keeping (coda
one_grief_shared, the war that never starts); without it the claim is met with the
old answers and shelved (coda claim_shelved, both wrong books standing). Endings
fire once per the D-112 rule; the settlings with the straddler pay the same 3
essence either way and never open unasked. Ten storylets in all.

**Save v58 to v59** (tier-2+ selection now draws among four, deep worlds re-deal),
and the bar held: 583 tests green (9 new FaithsTests: casting by office, both
aggressor sides occurring, the promise, the separate accounts, the socket with the
doorward's confession, both climaxes, ends-once, and the settlings with the cold
path paying nothing). Twins byte-identical and journals replaying exact through the
independent sim on all five sweep seeds (1, 7, 99, 2024, 88888), all reaching cycle
13. The redeal remapped the pinned fixture masters: blight 41 held, throne 40 to 7
(direct tier-2 seed 7 to 2), the stead's cycle-2 master 42 to 43, and the faiths
pinned at 44 (crossed and direct both). The repeat-weighting range widened for a
four-template pool (repeats near one seventh). The talk and rest beats are
pilot-unexercised and test-covered per the D-109/D-111 precedent, except the
doors beat, which fired live three times in the master's twelve worlds. Options set
aside: drawing the champions by lot (an office carries doctrine; a lottery would
cast a woodward as a faith's voice); a combat-deed climax (this template owns no
hostile site, and borrowing the camp's deed would hang a faith's ending on the
raids' story); waiting for player-choice machinery to stage the three-way ending
the full-scale spec sketches (the slice branches on found truth, the same honest
axis as the Blight and the Throne, and the choice-staged endings can join when
dialogue scenes exist). Deferred: the war-profiteer optional role; escalation
beats between the wrong and the claim-saying if the feud ever wants a longer fuse;
the coda facts feeding a future world's mythology weight once cross-world echoes
read codas.

### D-115: The two-faiths worldgen lane: the harrow raised, the keeper cast (2026-07-20)
D-114's first lane lands, built to its spec: the valley now holds both faiths as
institutions, in every world at every tier, ahead of the War of Faiths template.
The harrow (a new SiteKind and terrain, glyph 'A') stands up the valley on its own
seed stream: a fully authored peaceful room in the songhall's mold (no carve RNG,
no spawns, no chest), holding a tended fire and, at the east end, the mother-stone
beside an empty socket, so the room states the founding without a speaker. The
founding itself goes into generated history as a fact: the harrow held the holy
ground first, and the stead's shrine-stone came down off its ring, a daughter-stone,
lent by the harrow's telling and given outright by the stead's. Cast: an elder and a
doorward of the harrow (NpcKind.Harrower) beside its door, and a shrinekeeper
(NpcKind.Keeper) at the stead's shrine, placed at a diagonal shoulder, never a
cardinal, so the rest point's every straight approach and the column's road stay
clear (the all-8 placement failed two shrine tests immediately: a keeper standing in
the east doorway turned a test's step into a bump).

**The doctrine speaks on both sides, per D-114's calls.** The villagers' shared nine
digits are full in a deep world (eight topics plus a trade offer), so the stead's
side lives on the keeper, where it institutionally belongs: the keeping (shelter is
a gift; you do not bill a gift), the two readings named as readings, and the rumor
line (word at the well is the elder means to come down and say its claim at the
shrine itself). The elder carries the order's side: the founding as custody, and the
debt reading (the power holds an account as a river holds water; sweeping is
housekeeping, not payment). The doorward keeps shorter answers and the one sharpened
hint ("most years that is a difference for winter evenings. Most years."). The war,
the aggressor, and the schism accounts are deliberately absent: template-time casts,
per the spec.

**Save v57 to v58** (new site, new people, every world re-deals), and the D-114
verification bar held: 574 tests green (five new in HarrowTests: the site and both
faiths' folk in every world, folk on plain ground at every tier, twins, the two
voices with the rumor line, the room's founding surfaces), journey twins
byte-identical and journals replaying exact through the independent sim path on all
five sweep seeds (1, 7, 99, 2024, 88888), all reaching cycle 13.

**Two live finds along the way, both fixed.** First, the harrow's folk were cast on
any walkable neighbor, which can be another site's mouth, and the journey pilot
paths to a mouth as a goal, so a doorward on a door meant an endless bump-and-close:
folk now stand on plain ground only (grass, forest, hills), with a regression test.
Second, a latent D-100/D-104 pilot bug the re-deal exposed: seed 88888's cycle-10
world pinned the laden mule at Chebyshev 2 against houses and folk where its follow
step could not close the gap, and the bearer's ridden stride (two cells a key on
open grass) overshot the adjacency ring every key, orbiting its own saddlebags for
the whole 60000-key world budget. The pilot gained ApproachBeast: it simulates the
engine's own stride landing per direction and takes a key that ends beside the bags
(in the stuck geometry, the southeast step whose stride the smith's own standing
spot happens to block). Engine untouched by that fix; it is the pilot learning a
rule the engine already had. Options set aside: an unconditional villager topic for
the harrow (would breach the nine digits when an echo world fills all eight topic
slots, exactly the overflow the D-041 lesson warns about); making 'o' reach the bags
at stride range (a design change to D-100's "beside" for what is purely a pilot
blind spot). Deferred to the template lane (D-032/D-035/D-112 seam): eligibility,
casting, threads, endings, the two schism accounts, and whether the harrow's elder
becomes the named-figure candidate D-110 keeps deferring.

### D-114: The valley's two faiths: scoping the War of Faiths' institutions (2026-07-20)
Design-only, no code: the scoping the roadmap's "wants a second faith-bearing
institution first" clause called for, settled in conversation. The gap turned out
wider than the clause says: the template needs two *organized* faiths, and the world
holds one faith-anchor (the Aegis-shrine at the stead) that is not an institution,
because nobody keeps it as a vocation. So the lane firms up the first faith while
adding the second.

**Chosen shape (Option A with one borrow):** a new order with its own enterable
site, whose doctrine differs from the stead's folk practice over what the
shrine-power is and what is owed to it (same power, read differently). The borrow
buys the arc resonance the template doc asks for ("what is owed to a guiding power
that is not what it claims") while keeping both champions, both institutions, and
the founding site fully template-owned, so the debunking never lands on anything
the arc needs intact. Rejected: growing the faith out of the Severed thread (one
hermit cannot carry an order, and a Severed faith flirts with turning the arc echo
from flavor into dependency, which the template doc forbids); a dead-faith around
the long mound (contradicts D-113's judgment that the mound's dead are a condition,
not a cast: there is nobody there to be a believer-champion).

**Three calls made:** the order venerates the same power read differently (sharpest
doctrinal conflict, makes the shared founding natural); the stead's shrine gets a
dedicated keeper NPC role, like the smith and skald (the first faith becomes
literally an institution, and the keeper-of-the-founding-site slot gets a rooted
candidate); ambient presence in non-template worlds is light coloring only (topics
on both sides, a rumor line, the doctrinal difference audible in talk, no
mechanics), so the valley reads as holding two faiths without stage dressing or a
premature save-format commitment.

**Two lanes, in order:**
1. **The worldgen lane** (buildable next): a new SiteKind for the order's house at
   the old holy site up the valley (working name: the harrow, from the old word for
   a holy place; naming adjustable at build time). The shared founding goes literal
   in geography: the harrow is the elder site and the stead's shrine its daughter,
   so custody is a present-tense tension seed rather than a retrofitted fact. Cast:
   an elder at the harrow with one or two of its folk, and a keeper at the stead's
   shrine. A founding fact planted in generated history, topics on both sides, a
   rumor line. Deliberately NOT at worldgen: the war, the aggressor designation,
   and the two schism accounts, which are cast at template time per the template
   spec. Save bump expected (v57 to v58, new site and people redeal worlds), so the
   master and sweep baselines re-deal: the verification bar is twins identical,
   replay exact, and the journey completing on all sweep seeds, not byte-identity.
2. **The template lane** (after the institutions exist and verify): eligibility,
   casting, threads, and endings through the same compiler seam as the Blight and
   the Throne (D-032, D-035, D-112).

### D-113: The valley's memories read aloud: the roster follow-ons batch (2026-07-20)
A small consolidation lane after two template-scale sessions, cashing the deferred
follow-ons of D-106, D-109, D-110 and D-111 in one pass. Four pieces, all perception
and stead-voice, no new state, no save bump (v57 holds):
- **The mended page** (Talk, priority 8): the made_right fact's consumer in its turn,
  the roadmap's "stead remembering the one who made right." Deliberately not a clean
  slate: the stead keeps the mend where the young can see the stitching, because a
  stead's memory is its only wall. Fires on a later talk than the well's nod (it
  requires the fact that beat writes), so remembering reads as remembering. No coin,
  no regard: the D-109 discipline holds, restitution never turns a profit, and being
  well-remembered is the whole reward.
- **The two memories** (Talk, priority 9, above the mended page while both stand):
  the made_right thread meeting the roster's memory. Requires made_right AND
  nemesis/risen: a bearer who paid the stead's book shut while the dens' book passed
  open to a risen heir hears the valley's two ledgers read side by side, the heir
  named ({r1.object}). Epistemology kept: the villager speaks only what a stead can
  perceive (the fires' new voice, the hill's habit of keeping tallies); the Aegis is
  the one who reads the far book's owner aloud.
- **The mound topic reads the grudge aloud** (D-106 follow-on): while the mark
  stands, the long-mound topic escalates from the old unease to the pacing lights
  and the mound's tally ("a mound only stirs when something was carried off it").
  The bearer is never named; the dogs, who growl before the bearer reaches the
  gate, are the only ones in the stead who know more. Settles back through the
  cleared branch at the stilling.
- **The chief told apart on the map** (D-110 follow-on): the roster's leader draws
  capital among its lowercase raiders (G among g), same kind, same colors: rank,
  not a new monster. The eye gets what the ear already had from the rumor.
The conditional third roster item (a named figure for another faction "if one earns
its keep") was judged not yet earned and stays tracked: the mound has no individuals
to name (its dead are a condition, not a cast) and the stead's names are the NPCs
themselves; the next candidate is whatever institution the War of Faiths brings.
Verification: 568 tests green (2 new FactConsumerTests, 1 new MoundTests, 1 new
PresenterTests). Twins identical; the master key journal came out bit-identical to
the D-112 baseline and its sim replay exact (13 cycles / 11846 turns / 1 death /
12560 keys), which is the honest expectation: every piece is presentation or
stead-voice, and the two talk beats are shame-gated roads the pilot never walks
(it robs no doors), so they are pilot-unexercised and test-covered, the D-109/D-111
precedent.

### D-112: The Usurped Throne: the first faction-hungry template, cast on the dens (2026-07-20)
The roster's unblock cashed: the third compiled world-story (template 1 of
design/story/world-story-templates.md sec 5), and the spec's own clause that "throne
reads broadly" decided the casting, because at slice scale the only polity with a
personal ruler, a succession event in living memory, and a displaced line is the dens
themselves. D-110 built exactly those parts: the chief is the ruler-by-lie, a
lieutenant is cast as the old blood (the claimant), and the live succession machinery
plays the restoration beats without a line of new engine code. The world seed names a
dead chief from the story's own stream (no roster draw moves), and the accepted
history blames the taking outward: the dens' telling is that a stead arrow took the
old chief off the palisade, and the sitting chief rose swearing to collect that debt,
which is what makes the lie load-bearing: it keeps the raids righteous. The evidence
is a cairn behind the fires, deep as the dens' own sleeping-ground (threshold mirrored
from the spawn depth so it is always at least as far in as the deepest raider), and
the flip complicates rather than inverts, per the template contract: no arrow but a
den-blade under the ribs, and cairn-goods sworn for one night of fire against the
stead, so the usurper's knife bought the stead its standing walls and the lie that
hides the knife is the same lie that keeps the raids coming. Eight storylets: the
story told (teller-only, pre-deed, writes heard/seat_story: a story never asked for
cannot later be settled), the cairn (writes evidence/seat_truth), the line restored
and the line passed over (Talk beats riding nemesis/risen, reading the succession as
den-talk at the doors), two endings on the deed (codas seat_truth_carried /
seat_lie_stands), and two settlings with the teller, both paying 3 essence (endings
differ in what the world believes, never in coin) and both gated on having heard the
story (the cold path pays nothing, like both elder templates). Eligibility is tier 2+
with villagers and a camp, so the first world keeps its single crafted story and
tier-1 worlds consume the RNG they always did; tier-2+ selection now draws among
three, redealing every deep world, so the save bumps v56 -> v57 and the fixture
masters remapped (blight 42 -> 41, stead 43 -> 42, throne 40; tier-2 seed 7 tells the
throne directly). The pilot channel surfaced a real content hole before it shipped: in
two of the master's five throne worlds the camp fell before the cairn was read, the
lie ending closed the story, and the truth ending then fired off the NEXT deed's hook,
two endings in one world. The rule is now explicit in both evidence templates
(endings forbid their story's own story_complete): a story ends once, and a truth dug
up after the fall reaches the teller through the settling but does not rewrite the
fall. Options set aside: casting the throne on the stead (no ruler, no succession: the
steadholder is a host, not a seat) and waiting for a proper polity (a template that
waits for content nobody has scheduled is a template that never ships; the dens
version exercises every contract feature now and a grander casting can join the pool
later). Verified: 564 tests green (10 ThroneTests, 1 blight regression); twins
identical on master and all four sweep seeds; the master's key journal came out
bit-identical to D-111's (the pilot's course is story-agnostic on that seed: template
beats are lines and facts, and none of its decisions read them), its sim replay exact,
and the throne confirmed live in all five of its worlds from the full log: 5 cairns,
3 truth endings, 2 lie endings, no doubles; sweep journeys moved as their worlds
redealt (7600/7732/7501/7763 turns, deaths 1/6/2/1). Deferred: the War of Faiths
(wants a second faith-bearing institution the world does not yet hold); the roster
follow-ons unchanged from D-111.

### D-111: The roster read aloud, and the exits audited (2026-07-20)
Two small closures in one lane. First, the nemesis facts get their stead-side readers,
D-110's own named deferral, in the D-088 discipline (narrative only; the world reading
differently is the payoff). The goblin-raids talk topic now reads the dens' order live:
a risen chief is named at the doors ("a new voice over them since the old one fell"),
and a camp whose named have all fallen without an heir reads as leaderless ("leaderless
is not gone; it is only quieter about its plans"), both gated on the live roster plus
the graph, so the answer tracks the camp as it stands. The scar deliberately has no
stead-side reader: the stead speaks only what it can perceive, and no stead can see a
raider's wound or whose shape is on it; that memory stays between the bearer and the
scarred. Second, the kept boast comes home as a storylet (the-boast-come-home, Talk,
once per world, requires nemesis/slew_bearer): the stead heard the name in the howling
the night the bearer fell, and laughs the boast off to the standing bearer's face,
den-talk not being believed at the doors, so the truest boast the dens ever made is the
one the stead dismisses, and only the Aegis says the joke's other half out loud. This
keeps the stead's epistemology intact: it never learns the death held; it only fails to
believe a thing that was true. Third, the D-023 exit-conditions audit, and the box
closes: every live conflict was walked against the tick code and each has a designed
exit. The raids end at the camp-fall (CheckSiteCleared) with the bared lofts as their
own dark exit; the watch stands down at the greed-break, the camp-fall, or the empty
loft; the levy lifts by answers or the post-camp recovery; shame exits by restitution
into the made_right beat; the mound's grudge exits at the stilling with the risen cap
bounding the seething; boldness is derived and self-cowing; wrath deliberately never
decays (hate compounds) but its actors go extinct at the camp-fall and its only teeth
favor the bearer, so it can stall nothing; the nemesis grudges die with their holders
and are spoken once per memory; and every ledger resets at the crossing, the doctrine
backstop, because the folk are this world's. No eternal stalemate exists. No new state,
no save bump (v56 holds). 553 tests green (3 new NemesisTests: the risen voice in the
topic, the leaderless read, the boast at the well fired once). Twins identical; sim
replay exact; master baseline byte-identical (13 cycles, 11846 turns, 1 death), sweep
seeds 1/7/88888 identical and seed 99 moved as designed (10056 to 9918 turns, deaths
held at 1): its pilot dies to a named raider, and the boast storylet then wins a talk
draw, which the full sim log confirms live (the slaying line and the boast both fire
under the pilot on seed 99); the topic branches are pilot-unexercised (the pilot never
presses the raids digit) and test-covered. Options set aside: a stead-side scar reader
(breaks perceivability doctrine, above); the stead believing the boast and reckoning
with a returned dead bearer (a metaphysics reveal the stead is not written to carry;
the arc keeps death's truth between the bearer, the Aegis, and the arc's own cast);
wrath decay on quiet ticks (hate compounding is the axis' character, and the audit
shows it stalls nothing). Deferred, unchanged from D-110's list: the made_right thread
meeting the roster's memory; the chief told apart on the map; a named figure for
another faction if one earns its keep.

### D-110: The named of the dens: the Nemesis-style roster begun (2026-07-20)
D-023's last big structural clause lands where the fighting is. The research lesson
(research/08, Shadow of Mordor) has waited since the factions were decided: named-individual
memory, not aggregate numbers, is what makes a faction feel alive, and it is cheap at the
scale of a handful of tracked individuals. So the raider camp gets a bounded roster: every
world's seed names a chief and two lieutenants from a new roster stream (a raider weave in
NameGen, vowel-final openers bitten off by consonant closers, so no den-name sounds like a
stead), the stead's rumor carries the chief's name from the first morning beside a
nemesis/chief fact, and rank is worn as hide (+4/+2 Hp over the tier's base), so the chief
is a tougher goblin without a single new ability. The memory is the point, and it rides
the replay with nothing serialized and no save bump (v56 holds). Three beats, all
user-approved as recommended: the scar (a named raider bloodied under the bearer's visit
and left alive keeps the wound's author, swept when the bearer leaves by ladder or by
dying in the camp, nemesis/scarred written); the succession (a chief slain over a standing
lieutenant hands the camp to a named heir, the office coming with the grudge already in
it, nemesis/risen written, and the last named falling to the no-heir silence, so clearing
a camp reads as an escalation ladder that ends in quiet); and the slaying (the very hand
that authors the bearer's death, a new _deathHand beside D-098's _deathShape, keeps the
boast, nemesis/slew_bearer written, so meeting your own killer is a scene the game can
only have with names). Teeth stay light on the established rail: a held grudge arms the
hand one point after the dice (RaiderRoster.Armed, the dread's mirror, draw count
untouched), and every grudge is spoken to the bearer's face at the next descent, once per
memory, the slaying's boast outranking the scar outranking the risen office. The roster is
this world's alone, per the ledger doctrine: the next world's dens have not met the
bearer. Verified: 550 tests green (8 new NemesisTests), twins byte-identical, sim replay
